Read: elements/conifers/tree.py

Now I'll write the real growth model with element-based foliage (fronds, strands, brush, spurs), fans, reiterated leaders and spike tops.

Write: elements/conifers/grow.py
"""Grow the four Pacific Northwest conifers as geometry in metres.

Differences from tree.py (the stage-0 copy model, kept frozen as its regression):

- foliage is built from *elements* the way the real sprays hang, per species:
    frond  : a flat spray off the side of a twig, arcing down at its tip (hemlock lace, fir shelves)
    strand : a pendant branchlet or hanging fan (cedar curtains, Sitka's pendulous branchlets)
    brush  : a tuft of short needled twigs round the branch, drooping (Douglas-fir)
    spur   : the needled branch tip itself (the horizontal spur that makes the silhouette)
- mature and old trees fan each limb into secondary branches (flat shelves / clumps),
  old crowns have gaps, dead limbs, flattened or broken tops;
- western redcedar grows reiterated leaders (candelabra) and dead spike tops; hemlock nods.
- wood (bole, limbs, dead spikes) is a list of tapered segments the painter samples as surfaces.

All points carry: p (position), n (normal), d (stroke direction), pad (across, vertical, along),
bid (branch id), eid (element id), ek (0..1 position along its element), kind (element type).
y is up; x right; z toward the viewer.
"""
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, replace

GOLD = np.pi * (3 - np.sqrt(5))
KIND = {"frond": 0, "strand": 1, "brush": 2, "spur": 3, "leader": 4}


@dataclass
class Sp:
    name: str
    crown_pow: float = 1.0
    crown_ratio: float = 0.65
    rmax_frac: float = 0.18
    whorled: bool = True
    per_whorl: tuple = (4, 6)
    internode: float = 0.5
    base_angle: float = 0.0
    top_upsweep: float = 0.6
    low_droop: float = 0.3
    droop: float = 0.3
    tip_upturn: float = 0.0
    len_jitter: float = 0.2
    fans: float = 1.0            # secondary branches per limb (scaled by age)
    fan_angle: float = 0.7       # radians off the limb, horizontal
    spray_start: float = 0.3
    # elements: weight per metre of foliated twig (per side) and sizes in metres
    frond_w: float = 0.0
    frond_len: float = 0.5
    frond_droop: float = 0.4
    frond_fwd: float = 0.7        # angle forward toward the twig tip (rad)
    pinna: float = 0.25           # pinna length / frond length (lacy width)
    strand_w: float = 0.0
    strand_len: float = 0.5
    strand_fan: int = 1
    brush_w: float = 0.0
    brush_len: float = 0.25
    spacing: float = 0.12         # metres between element anchors along a twig
    leader_nod: float = 0.0
    buttress: float = 0.0
    flutes: int = 0
    trunk_frac: float = 0.012
    bark: str = "furrow"
    candelabra: float = 0.0       # chance of reiterated leaders when old
    spike: float = 0.0            # chance of a dead spike top when old
    shelf: float = 0.5            # how flat the shelves are (1 = flat tiers, 0 = rounded clumps)


SP = {
    "douglas": Sp("douglas", crown_pow=0.9, crown_ratio=0.62, rmax_frac=0.16, whorled=True,
                  per_whorl=(3, 5), internode=0.7, base_angle=0.1, top_upsweep=0.8, low_droop=0.5,
                  droop=0.28, tip_upturn=0.12, len_jitter=0.3, fans=3.0, fan_angle=0.8,
                  spray_start=0.25, brush_w=1.0, brush_len=0.3, strand_w=0.35, strand_len=0.35,
                  strand_fan=1, spacing=0.11, trunk_frac=0.016, bark="furrow", buttress=0.25,
                  shelf=0.35, candelabra=0.1, spike=0.15),
    "hemlock": Sp("hemlock", crown_pow=0.9, crown_ratio=0.72, rmax_frac=0.15, whorled=False,
                  per_whorl=(1, 2), internode=0.3, base_angle=-0.1, top_upsweep=0.4, low_droop=0.6,
                  droop=0.45, tip_upturn=0.0, len_jitter=0.3, fans=2.5, fan_angle=0.9,
                  spray_start=0.2, frond_w=1.0, frond_len=0.45, frond_droop=0.7, frond_fwd=0.8,
                  pinna=0.3, strand_w=0.2, strand_len=0.3, spacing=0.1, leader_nod=0.05,
                  trunk_frac=0.012, bark="scaly", buttress=0.2, shelf=0.6, spike=0.05),
    "cedar": Sp("cedar", crown_pow=0.8, crown_ratio=0.75, rmax_frac=0.18, whorled=False,
                per_whorl=(1, 2), internode=0.32, base_angle=-0.35, top_upsweep=0.9, low_droop=0.5,
                droop=0.3, tip_upturn=0.55, len_jitter=0.3, fans=2.0, fan_angle=0.6,
                spray_start=0.15, frond_w=0.35, frond_len=0.4, frond_droop=0.3, frond_fwd=0.5,
                pinna=0.35, strand_w=1.0, strand_len=0.7, strand_fan=3, spacing=0.13,
                leader_nod=0.03, trunk_frac=0.02, bark="fibrous", buttress=0.9, flutes=7,
                shelf=0.3, candelabra=0.8, spike=0.7),
    "spruce": Sp("spruce", crown_pow=1.0, crown_ratio=0.72, rmax_frac=0.21, whorled=True,
                 per_whorl=(5, 7), internode=0.55, base_angle=0.0, top_upsweep=0.6, low_droop=0.45,
                 droop=0.3, tip_upturn=0.35, len_jitter=0.2, fans=2.5, fan_angle=0.7,
                 spray_start=0.25, frond_w=0.5, frond_len=0.3, frond_droop=0.15, frond_fwd=0.6,
                 pinna=0.2, strand_w=0.8, strand_len=0.5, strand_fan=1, spacing=0.12,
                 trunk_frac=0.018, bark="plates", buttress=0.6, flutes=3, shelf=0.7,
                 candelabra=0.15, spike=0.1),
}

AGE = {
    #          height  crown  irr   dead  gaps  girth fanmul
    "sapling": dict(H=2.5, cr=1.0, irr=0.1, dead=0.0, gaps=0.0, girth=1.8, fan=0.0),
    "young":   dict(H=13., cr=0.95, irr=0.2, dead=0.1, gaps=0.0, girth=1.0, fan=0.3),
    "mature":  dict(H=40., cr=0.62, irr=0.45, dead=0.25, gaps=0.12, girth=1.0, fan=0.8),
    "old":     dict(H=65., cr=0.45, irr=0.85, dead=0.4, gaps=0.3, girth=1.45, fan=1.0),
}


@dataclass
class Tree:
    sp: Sp
    age: str
    H: float
    trunk: np.ndarray            # (n,4) x y z r
    wood: list                   # segments: dict(a (3,), b (3,), ra, rb, kind 'bark'|'dead'|'limb')
    branches: list
    fol: dict
    crown_base: float
    axis: np.ndarray
    rfun: object
    top_y: float                 # top of live crown
    moss: float = 0.0


def crown_radius(sp, t, rmax, round_top):
    t = np.clip(t, 0, 1)
    r = (1 - t) ** sp.crown_pow
    r = r * np.clip(0.6 + 0.4 * np.minimum(t / 0.2, 1.0), 0, 1)
    if round_top > 0:
        rt = np.sqrt(np.clip(1 - t ** 2, 0, 1)) * 0.8
        r = (1 - round_top) * r + round_top * np.maximum(r, rt * (t < 0.985))
    return rmax * r


def grow(species="douglas", age="mature", seed=0, H=None, openness=0.6, lean=(0.0, 0.0), mpp=0.1,
         moss=0.0, detail=1.0, **over):
    sp = SP[species] if isinstance(species, str) else species
    if over:
        sp = replace(sp, **{k: v for k, v in over.items() if hasattr(sp, k)})
    ag = AGE[age]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed * 7919 + hash(sp.name) % 1000)
    H = H or ag["H"] * rng.uniform(0.85, 1.15)
    irr = ag["irr"]
    cr = min(0.97, sp.crown_ratio * ag["cr"] * (0.75 + 0.5 * openness)) if age != "sapling" else 0.93
    cb = H * (1 - cr)
    rmax = sp.rmax_frac * H * (0.85 if age == "old" else 1.0) * rng.uniform(0.88, 1.12)
    if age == "sapling":
        rmax = sp.rmax_frac * H * 1.5
    rmax *= (0.8 + 0.4 * openness)
    round_top = {"sapling": 0, "young": 0, "mature": 0.12, "old": 0.55}[age]
    if sp.name == "hemlock":
        round_top *= 0.5

    # ---------------- trunk
    ny = 90
    ys = np.linspace(0, H, ny)
    lean = np.array(lean, float)
    sw = rng.normal(0, 1, 4)
    xs = lean[0] * ys + 0.005 * H * irr * np.sin(ys / H * np.pi * (1 + abs(sw[0])) + sw[1])
    zs = lean[1] * ys + 0.005 * H * irr * np.sin(ys / H * np.pi * (1 + abs(sw[2])) + sw[3])
    dbh = sp.trunk_frac * H * ag["girth"]
    if age == "sapling":
        dbh = 0.02 + 0.01 * H
    rs = dbh * (1 - ys / H) ** (0.75 if age == "old" else 0.9) + 0.01
    flare = sp.buttress * dbh * (1.7 if age == "old" else 1.0) * np.exp(-ys / (0.035 * H + 0.5))
    rs = rs + flare * (age != "sapling")
    trunk = np.stack([xs, ys, zs, rs], 1)
    if sp.leader_nod > 0:
        tl = sp.leader_nod * H * (1.3 if age in ("young", "sapling") else 1.0)
        k = ys > H - tl
        u = (ys[k] - (H - tl)) / tl
        trunk[k, 0] += (u ** 2) * tl * 0.8
        trunk[k, 1] -= (u ** 2.2) * tl * 0.45

    def at(y):
        return np.array([np.interp(y, trunk[:, 1], trunk[:, 0]), y, np.interp(y, trunk[:, 1], trunk[:, 2])])

    wood = []
    # spike top (dead leader) for old cedar etc.
    spike_top = 0.0
    if age == "old" and rng.random() < sp.spike:
        spike_top = rng.uniform(0.04, 0.12) * H
    live_top = H - spike_top
    for i in range(ny - 1):
        a = trunk[i]; b = trunk[i + 1]
        kind = "dead" if a[1] > live_top else "bark"
        wood.append(dict(a=a[:3].copy(), b=b[:3].copy(), ra=a[3], rb=b[3], kind=kind, trunk=True))

    # ---------------- limbs
    internode = max(sp.internode * (H / 20.0) ** 0.5, 0.1)
    levels = []
    y = cb
    while y < live_top * 0.985:
        levels.append(y)
        y += internode * rng.uniform(0.7, 1.3)
    branches = []
    az0 = rng.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi)
    ai = 0
    lob = rng.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi, 4)
    # old crowns: a few big "clumps" of limbs at irregular heights
    for li, y in enumerate(levels):
        t = (y - cb) / (live_top - cb)
        n = rng.integers(sp.per_whorl[0], sp.per_whorl[1] + 1)
        rr = crown_radius(sp, t, rmax, round_top)
        for j in range(n):
            if sp.whorled:
                az = az0 + li * 0.7 + j * 2 * np.pi / n + rng.normal(0, 0.3)
            else:
                ai += 1
                az = az0 + ai * GOLD * 2 + rng.normal(0, 0.4)
            lobe = 1 + 0.25 * irr * np.sin(az * 3 + lob[0] + t * 4) + 0.2 * irr * np.sin(az * 2 + lob[1] - t * 3)
            L = rr * lobe * rng.uniform(1 - sp.len_jitter - 0.3 * irr, 1 + 0.4 * sp.len_jitter)
            if rng.random() < ag["gaps"] * (1.2 - t):
                continue
            if L < 0.04:
                continue
            ang = sp.base_angle + sp.top_upsweep * max(0, t - 0.55) * 2.2 - sp.low_droop * max(0, 0.5 - t) * 2
            ang += rng.normal(0, 0.07 + 0.12 * irr)
            dead = t < ag["dead"] and rng.random() < 0.6 * (1 - t / max(ag["dead"], 1e-3))
            b = _limb(at(y), az, ang, L, sp, rng, t, dead, H, age)
            branches.append(b)
            # fans: secondary branches spread off the limb
            nf = int(round(sp.fans * ag["fan"] * rng.uniform(0.5, 1.3) * min(1, L / 1.5)))
            if not dead:
                for k in range(nf):
                    s0 = rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75)
                    side = 1 if k % 2 == 0 else -1
                    p0 = _pt(b["pts"], s0)
                    az2 = az + side * sp.fan_angle * rng.uniform(0.6, 1.3)
                    L2 = (1 - s0) * L * rng.uniform(0.55, 0.95)
                    ang2 = ang * 0.5 + rng.normal(0, 0.1) - 0.1 * sp.droop
                    c = _limb(p0, az2, ang2, L2, sp, rng, t, False, H, age, child=True)
                    c["parent"] = len(branches) - 1
                    branches.append(c)
    # dead stubs on the bole below the crown
    if ag["dead"] > 0 and cb > 3:
        y = cb * rng.uniform(0.3, 0.6)
        while y < cb:
            az = rng.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi)
            L = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0) * (H / 40) + 0.15
            branches.append(_limb(at(y), az, rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.1), L, sp, rng, 0.0, True, H, age))
            y += internode * rng.uniform(2, 6) * (2 if age == "old" else 1)
    # candelabra: reiterated leaders from upper limbs (old cedar mostly)
    reits = []
    if age == "old" and rng.random() < sp.candelabra:
        nre = rng.integers(2, 5)
        for k in range(nre):
            y0 = live_top - rng.uniform(0.08, 0.3) * (live_top - cb)
            az = rng.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi)
            p0 = at(y0)
            off = rng.uniform(0.5, 1.2) * crown_radius(sp, (y0 - cb) / (live_top - cb), rmax, round_top) * 0.5
            hre = rng.uniform(0.12, 0.25) * H
            dead_re = rng.random() < 0.45
            top = p0 + np.array([np.cos(az) * off, hre, np.sin(az) * off])
            mid = p0 + np.array([np.cos(az) * off * 0.9, hre * 0.35, np.sin(az) * off * 0.9])
            r0 = dbh * 0.25
            wood.append(dict(a=p0, b=mid, ra=r0, rb=r0 * 0.7, kind="bark", trunk=False))
            wood.append(dict(a=mid, b=top, ra=r0 * 0.7, rb=0.03, kind="dead" if dead_re else "bark", trunk=False))
            if not dead_re:
                reits.append((mid, top))
                # small crown on the reiteration
                for yy in np.arange(mid[1], top[1] - 0.3, internode):
                    u = (yy - mid[1]) / (top[1] - mid[1])
                    pp = mid + (top - mid) * u
                    for j in range(rng.integers(1, 3)):
                        az3 = rng.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi)
                        L3 = (1 - u) * rmax * 0.35 * rng.uniform(0.6, 1.2) + 0.2
                        branches.append(_limb(pp, az3, sp.base_angle, L3, sp, rng, 0.8, False, H, age))
    # limb wood segments (thick enough ones are drawn by the painter)
    for bi, b in enumerate(branches):
        pts = b["pts"]
        for i in range(len(pts) - 1):
            s = i / (len(pts) - 1)
            wood.append(dict(a=pts[i], b=pts[i + 1], ra=b["r"] * (1 - s * 0.8), rb=b["r"] * (1 - (s + 1 / (len(pts) - 1)) * 0.8),
                             kind="dead" if b["dead"] else "limb", trunk=False, bid=bi))

    fol = _foliage(branches, sp, rng, mpp, detail, H, age)
    # needled leader(s)
    tops = [(at(live_top - max(0.07 * H, 3 * mpp)), at(live_top * 0.999))] if spike_top == 0 else []
    tops += [(m + (t - m) * 0.7, t) for m, t in reits]
    for a, b in tops:
        _leader(fol, a, b, mpp, rng)
    axis = np.stack([at(y) for y in np.linspace(cb, live_top, 24)])
    rf = lambda yy: crown_radius(sp, (yy - cb) / (live_top - cb), rmax, round_top)
    return Tree(sp, age, H, trunk, wood, branches, fol, cb, axis, rf, live_top, moss)


def _pt(pts, s):
    idx = s * (len(pts) - 1)
    i0 = int(np.clip(np.floor(idx), 0, len(pts) - 2))
    f = idx - i0
    return pts[i0] * (1 - f) + pts[i0 + 1] * f


def _limb(p0, az, ang, L, sp, rng, t, dead, H, age, child=False):
    s = np.linspace(0, 1, 14)
    d = np.array([np.cos(az), 0, np.sin(az)])
    rise = np.sin(ang) * s * L
    sag = sp.droop * L * s ** 2 * (1 - 0.3 * t) * (1.25 if age in ("old", "mature") else 1)
    up = sp.tip_upturn * L * np.clip(s - 0.5, 0, 1) ** 2 * 3.2
    horiz = np.cos(ang) * s * L
    wob = rng.normal(0, 0.03 * L, (14, 3)).cumsum(0) * 0.25
    wob[:, 1] *= 0.5
    pts = p0[None] + horiz[:, None] * d[None] + np.array([0, 1.0, 0])[None] * (rise - sag + up)[:, None] + wob * s[:, None]
    r0 = max(0.015, (0.022 if not child else 0.016) * L ** 1.1 + (0.02 if age == "old" else 0.0))
    return dict(pts=pts, r=r0, tier=t, dead=dead, az=az, L=L, dir=d, child=child)


def _foliage(branches, sp, rng, mpp, detail, H, age):
    out = {k: [] for k in ("p", "n", "d", "pad", "bid", "eid", "ek", "kind")}
    step = max(0.45 * mpp / detail, 0.015)
    eid = 0
    has_child = set(b.get("parent", -1) for b in branches)
    for bi, b in enumerate(branches):
        if b["dead"]:
            continue
        L = b["L"]; pts = b["pts"]
        s0 = sp.spray_start if not b["child"] else 0.1
        if bi in has_child:
            s0 = max(s0, 0.55)       # a fanned limb carries foliage mostly on its children and tip
        flen = (1 - s0) * L
        spacing = max(sp.spacing, 0.5 * mpp)
        na = max(2, int(flen / spacing))
        us = np.sort(rng.uniform(s0, 1.0, na))
        c = np.array([_pt(pts, u) for u in us])
        tang = np.gradient(c, axis=0) if na > 2 else np.tile(pts[-1] - pts[0], (na, 1))
        tang /= np.linalg.norm(tang, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9
        side = np.cross(tang, [0, 1.0, 0]); side /= np.linalg.norm(side, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9
        radial = b["dir"]
        # element size tapers toward the twig tip (young growth at the tip is short)
        taper = np.clip(1.15 - 0.6 * ((us - s0) / max(1 - s0, 1e-3)) ** 2, 0.4, 1.2)
        wsum = sp.frond_w + sp.strand_w + sp.brush_w
        for i in range(na):
            r = rng.random() * wsum
            kind = "frond" if r < sp.frond_w else ("strand" if r < sp.frond_w + sp.strand_w else "brush")
            for sgn in (-1, 1):
                if kind == "strand" and sgn == 1 and rng.random() < 0.5:
                    continue
                el = _element(kind, c[i], tang[i], side[i] * sgn, radial, sp, rng, step, taper[i], L)
                if el is None:
                    continue
                P, Nn, D, ek = el
                m = len(P)
                out["p"].append(P); out["n"].append(Nn); out["d"].append(D)
                out["ek"].append(ek)
                across = np.full(m, sgn * 0.5)
                vert = (P[:, 1] - c[i][1]) / (sp.strand_len + sp.frond_len + 0.1)
                out["pad"].append(np.stack([across, vert, np.full(m, us[i])], 1))
                out["bid"].append(np.full(m, bi)); out["eid"].append(np.full(m, eid)); out["kind"].append(np.full(m, KIND[kind]))
                eid += 1
        # spur: the needled twig tip, reads as the horizontal spur at the silhouette
        ns = max(2, int(0.35 * L / step))
        su = np.linspace(0.65, 1.0, ns)
        P = np.array([_pt(pts, u) for u in su]) + rng.normal(0, step * 0.3, (ns, 3))
        Dd = np.tile(tang[-1], (ns, 1))
        Nn = np.tile(np.array([0, 0.85, 0]) + 0.5 * radial, (ns, 1))
        Nn /= np.linalg.norm(Nn, axis=1, keepdims=True)
        out["p"].append(P); out["n"].append(Nn); out["d"].append(Dd); out["ek"].append((su - 0.65) / 0.35)
        out["pad"].append(np.stack([np.zeros(ns), np.full(ns, 0.2), su], 1))
        out["bid"].append(np.full(ns, bi)); out["eid"].append(np.full(ns, eid)); out["kind"].append(np.full(ns, KIND["spur"]))
        eid += 1
    fol = {k: (np.concatenate(v) if v else np.zeros((0, 3))) for k, v in out.items()}
    return fol


def _element(kind, c, tang, side, radial, sp, rng, step, taper, L):
    """Sample one foliage element as points. Returns (P, N, D, ek)."""
    if kind == "frond":
        ln = sp.frond_len * taper * rng.uniform(0.7, 1.25)
        fwd = sp.frond_fwd * rng.uniform(0.7, 1.2)
        dirv = side * np.cos(fwd) + tang * np.sin(fwd)
        n = max(2, int(ln / step) + 1)
        t = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
        dr = sp.frond_droop * rng.uniform(0.6, 1.3)
        P = c + np.outer(t * ln, dirv) - np.outer(dr * ln * t ** 2, [0, 1.0, 0])
        tg = np.gradient(P, axis=0); tg /= np.linalg.norm(tg, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9
        pts = [P]; ds = [tg]; eks = [t]
        # pinnae: short lateral twigs alternating, drooping; only where they are > half a pixel
        pl = sp.pinna * ln
        if pl > step * 1.2:
            npin = max(1, int(ln / (pl * 0.9)))
            for k in range(npin):
                tk = (k + 0.5) / npin
                base = c + dirv * tk * ln - np.array([0, 1.0, 0]) * dr * ln * tk ** 2
                for sg in (-1, 1):
                    pdir = dirv * 0.5 + np.cross(dirv, [0, 1.0, 0]) * sg
                    pdir /= np.linalg.norm(pdir) + 1e-9
                    m = max(1, int(pl * (1 - 0.5 * tk) / step))
                    tt = np.linspace(0, 1, m + 1)[1:]
                    Pp = base + np.outer(tt * pl * (1 - 0.5 * tk), pdir) - np.outer(tt * pl * 0.5 * dr, [0, 1.0, 0])
                    pts.append(Pp); ds.append(np.tile(pdir - [0, 0.4 * dr, 0], (m, 1))); eks.append(np.full(m, tk))
        P = np.concatenate(pts); D = np.concatenate(ds); ek = np.concatenate(eks)
        nrm = np.array([0, 1.0, 0]) + 0.35 * side + 0.25 * radial
        Nn = np.tile(nrm / np.linalg.norm(nrm), (len(P), 1))
        # the drooping outer part of the frond turns its face outward/down
        Nn = Nn + np.outer(ek ** 2 * dr, dirv) - np.outer(ek ** 2 * dr * 0.6, [0, 1.0, 0])
        Nn /= np.linalg.norm(Nn, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9
        return P, Nn, D, ek
    if kind == "strand":
        ln = sp.strand_len * taper * rng.uniform(0.6, 1.3)
        nf = sp.strand_fan
        pts, ds, eks, ns = [], [], [], []
        for f in range(nf):
            spread = (f - (nf - 1) / 2) * 0.35
            dirv = np.array([0, -1.0, 0]) + side * (0.25 + spread) + tang * 0.15 + radial * 0.1
            dirv /= np.linalg.norm(dirv)
            n = max(2, int(ln / step) + 1)
            t = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
            P = c + np.outer(t * ln, dirv) + np.outer(t ** 2 * ln * 0.15, radial)
            pts.append(P); ds.append(np.tile(dirv, (n, 1))); eks.append(t)
        P = np.concatenate(pts); D = np.concatenate(ds); ek = np.concatenate(eks)
        nrm = radial * 0.8 + side * 0.4 + np.array([0, 0.25, 0])
        Nn = np.tile(nrm / np.linalg.norm(nrm), (len(P), 1))
        return P, Nn, D, ek
    if kind == "brush":
        ln = sp.brush_len * taper * rng.uniform(0.7, 1.3)
        k = rng.integers(3, 6)
        pts, ds, eks = [], [], []
        for j in range(k):
            a = rng.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi)
            up = np.array([0, 1.0, 0])
            dirv = side * np.cos(a) * 0.8 + up * np.sin(a) * 0.6 + tang * 0.6 - up * 0.35
            dirv /= np.linalg.norm(dirv)
            n = max(1, int(ln / step) + 1)
            t = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
            P = c + side * 0.03 + np.outer(t * ln, dirv) - np.outer(t ** 2 * ln * 0.2, up)
            pts.append(P); ds.append(np.tile(dirv, (n, 1))); eks.append(t)
        P = np.concatenate(pts); D = np.concatenate(ds); ek = np.concatenate(eks)
        rel = P - c
        nrm = rel / (np.linalg.norm(rel, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-6) * 0.5 + np.array([0, 0.8, 0]) + 0.3 * radial
        Nn = nrm / np.linalg.norm(nrm, axis=1, keepdims=True)
        return P, Nn, D, ek
    return None


def _leader(fol, a, b, mpp, rng):
    ln = np.linalg.norm(b - a)
    n = max(3, int(ln / (0.35 * mpp)))
    t = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
    P = a + np.outer(t, b - a) + rng.normal(0, 0.2 * mpp, (n, 3)) * np.array([1, 0, 1])
    # whorl of tiny ticks near the top: crosses
    fol["p"] = np.concatenate([fol["p"], P])
    fol["n"] = np.concatenate([fol["n"], np.tile([0, 0.6, 0.8], (n, 1))])
    fol["d"] = np.concatenate([fol["d"], np.tile([0, -1.0, 0], (n, 1))])
    fol["pad"] = np.concatenate([fol["pad"], np.tile([0, 0.5, 1.0], (n, 1))])
    fol["bid"] = np.concatenate([fol["bid"], np.full(n, -1)])
    fol["eid"] = np.concatenate([fol["eid"], np.full(n, -1)])
    fol["ek"] = np.concatenate([fol["ek"], t])
    fol["kind"] = np.concatenate([fol["kind"], np.full(n, KIND["leader"])])
Write: elements/conifers/paint.py
"""The conifer painter: grow.Tree -> index canvas (material, step) -> colours.

Layer by layer (SYNTHESIS's scaffold, with the conifer techniques found in stages 0-4):
 1 form field   per foliage point: crown normal (cone about the stem) blended with the element's own
                normal, an opacity shadow map along the sun, crown-depth occlusion, a shelf term
                (top of each tier lit, underside dark)
 2 value plan   two families kept apart at a light threshold (lit / shadow)
 3 marks        every point is a 1-px body mark; a subset are stroke marks along the element
                direction (fronds droop, strands hang, brush tufts splay, spurs run out)
 4 quantize     a stochastic *mixture* of three steps per family whose proportions carry the form
                (stage 0: Ferrari's mid-distance conifer is black 47% / dark olive 23% / lit 27%,
                mean runs ~1.5 px)
 5 wood         bole, limbs, dead spikes sampled as surfaces; bark patterns per species designed
                in pixels (furrows, fibrous strips, scaly plates), buttress flutes
Materials: 0 none, 1 foliage, 2 bark, 3 limb, 4 dead (silver) wood, 5 moss.
"""
import numpy as np
from scipy.ndimage import uniform_filter, gaussian_filter

NONE, FOL, BARK, LIMB, DEAD, MOSS = 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5


def sun_vec(az_deg, el_deg):
    """az: 0 = sun on the viewer's left, 90 = sun in front (behind the viewer), 180 = right,
    270 = behind the tree (contre-jour)."""
    a = np.radians(az_deg); e = np.radians(el_deg)
    return np.array([-np.cos(a) * np.cos(e), np.sin(e), np.sin(a) * np.cos(e)])


class Cam:
    def __init__(self, mpp, el_deg=0.0, yaw_deg=0.0):
        self.mpp = mpp; self.el = np.radians(el_deg); self.yaw = np.radians(yaw_deg)

    def rot(self, p):
        c, s = np.cos(self.yaw), np.sin(self.yaw)
        return p[..., 0] * c + p[..., 2] * s, p[..., 1], -p[..., 0] * s + p[..., 2] * c

    def project(self, p):
        x, y, z = self.rot(p)
        ce, se = np.cos(self.el), np.sin(self.el)
        return x / self.mpp, -(y * ce - z * se) / self.mpp, y * se + z * ce

    def rotvec(self, d):
        x, y, z = self.rot(d)
        ce, se = np.cos(self.el), np.sin(self.el)
        return x, -(y * ce - z * se)

    def view(self):
        """unit vector from the scene toward the viewer (world coords)."""
        ce, se = np.cos(self.el), np.sin(self.el)
        v = np.array([0, se, ce])
        c, s = np.cos(self.yaw), np.sin(self.yaw)
        return np.array([v[0] * c - v[2] * s, v[1], v[0] * s + v[2] * c])


def shadow_T(p, L, cell, k):
    up = np.array([0, 1.0, 0]) if abs(L[1]) < 0.95 else np.array([1.0, 0, 0])
    e1 = np.cross(L, up); e1 /= np.linalg.norm(e1)
    e2 = np.cross(L, e1)
    u = p @ e1; v = p @ e2; w = p @ L
    iu = np.floor((u - u.min()) / cell).astype(np.int64)
    iv = np.floor((v - v.min()) / cell).astype(np.int64)
    nu, nv = iu.max() + 1, iv.max() + 1
    key = iu * nv + iv
    order = np.lexsort((-w, key))
    ks = key[order]
    start = np.r_[0, np.flatnonzero(np.diff(ks)) + 1]
    counts = np.diff(np.r_[start, len(ks)])
    rank = np.arange(len(ks)) - np.repeat(start, counts)
    before = np.empty(len(p)); before[order] = rank
    grid = np.zeros((nu, nv)); np.add.at(grid, (iu, iv), 1)
    gb = uniform_filter(grid, 3)
    frac = before / np.maximum(grid[iu, iv], 1)
    before = frac * gb[iu, iv] * 0.6 + before * 0.4
    return np.exp(-k * before)


def light_foliage(tr, L, mpp, P):
    f = tr.fol
    p, n = f["p"], f["n"]
    ax = tr.axis
    axx = np.interp(p[:, 1], ax[:, 1], ax[:, 0]); axz = np.interp(p[:, 1], ax[:, 1], ax[:, 2])
    rad = np.stack([p[:, 0] - axx, np.zeros(len(p)), p[:, 2] - axz], 1)
    rdist = np.linalg.norm(rad, axis=1)
    rad /= rdist[:, None] + 1e-9
    rf = np.maximum(tr.rfun(np.minimum(p[:, 1], tr.top_y)), 0.05)
    crown_len = tr.top_y - tr.crown_base
    nc = rad * np.cos(0.35) + np.array([0, np.sin(0.35), 0])
    topz = np.clip((p[:, 1] - (tr.top_y - 0.15 * crown_len)) / (0.15 * crown_len), 0, 1)
    nc[:, 1] += topz * 1.2
    nc /= np.linalg.norm(nc, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    wc = P.get("wc", 0.5)
    N = wc * nc + (1 - wc) * n
    N /= np.linalg.norm(N, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    wrap = P.get("wrap", 0.15)                          # translucent needles: light wraps a little
    lam = np.clip((N @ L + wrap) / (1 + wrap), 0, 1)
    cell = max(mpp * 2.0, 0.3)
    step = max(0.45 * mpp, 0.015)
    k = P.get("shadow_k", 0.5) * step ** 2 / cell ** 2
    T = shadow_T(p, L, cell, k)
    depth = np.clip(1 - rdist / rf, 0, 1)
    ao = np.clip(1 - depth * P.get("ao_k", 1.1), 0.04, 1) ** 1.2
    # shelf: the element's vertical position under its twig; tops of tiers lit, undersides dark
    vert = f["pad"][:, 1]
    ao *= np.clip(0.85 + P.get("shelf_k", 0.9) * vert, 0.25, 1.15)
    direct = lam * T
    amb = P.get("sky", 0.35) * ao * (0.55 + 0.45 * N[:, 1])
    return direct, amb, T, ao, N


def rasterize(tr, cam, L, W, H, origin, P):
    mpp = cam.mpp
    rng = np.random.default_rng(P.get("seed", 0) + 17)
    f = tr.fol
    G = dict(mat=np.zeros((H, W), np.int16), dep=np.full((H, W), -1e9), dir=np.zeros((H, W)),
             amb=np.zeros((H, W)), k=np.zeros((H, W), np.int16), mark=np.zeros((H, W), bool),
             eid=np.full((H, W), -1, np.int64), kind=np.full((H, W), -1, np.int16), ek=np.zeros((H, W)),
             u=np.zeros((H, W)), v=np.zeros((H, W)), T=np.ones((H, W)))
    if len(f["p"]):
        direct, amb, T, ao, N = light_foliage(tr, L, mpp, P)
        sx, sy, dep = cam.project(f["p"])
        sx = sx + origin[0]; sy = sy + origin[1]
        n = len(sx)
        mark_m = P.get("mark_m", 0.25)
        Ls = float(np.clip(mark_m / mpp, 1, P.get("max_stroke", 4)))
        slen = np.clip(np.round(Ls * rng.uniform(0.6, 1.3, n)), 1, P.get("max_stroke", 4))
        is_mark = rng.random(n) < P.get("mark_frac", 0.35)
        slen = np.where(is_mark, slen, 1)
        dx, dy = cam.rotvec(f["d"])
        nr = np.sqrt(dx ** 2 + dy ** 2) + 1e-9
        dx /= nr; dy /= nr
        XS, YS, DS, K, ID = [], [], [], [], []
        ids = np.arange(n)
        eps = P.get("mark_eps", 0.1)
        for k in range(int(slen.max())):
            m = slen > k
            XS.append(np.floor(sx[m] + dx[m] * k + 0.5).astype(np.int64))
            YS.append(np.floor(sy[m] + dy[m] * k + 0.5).astype(np.int64))
            DS.append(dep[m] + np.where(is_mark[m], eps, 0) - k * 1e-4)
            K.append(np.full(m.sum(), k, np.int16)); ID.append(ids[m])
        xs = np.concatenate(XS); ys = np.concatenate(YS); ds = np.concatenate(DS)
        kk = np.concatenate(K); pid = np.concatenate(ID)
        ok = (xs >= 0) & (xs < W) & (ys >= 0) & (ys < H)
        xs, ys, ds, kk, pid = xs[ok], ys[ok], ds[ok], kk[ok], pid[ok]
        o = np.argsort(ds, kind="stable")
        xs, ys, ds, kk, pid = xs[o], ys[o], ds[o], kk[o], pid[o]
        G["mat"][ys, xs] = FOL
        G["dep"][ys, xs] = ds
        G["dir"][ys, xs] = direct[pid]
        G["amb"][ys, xs] = amb[pid]
        G["k"][ys, xs] = kk
        G["mark"][ys, xs] = is_mark[pid]
        G["eid"][ys, xs] = f["eid"][pid]
        G["kind"][ys, xs] = f["kind"][pid]
        G["ek"][ys, xs] = f["ek"][pid]
        G["T"][ys, xs] = T[pid]
    _wood(tr, cam, L, G, origin, P)
    return G


def _wood(tr, cam, L, G, origin, P):
    """Tapered segments sampled on their viewer-facing half; the bole inside the crown sits in the
    crown's shade; limbs under a pixel wide are drawn as 1-px lines only where not covered."""
    mpp = cam.mpp
    H, W = G["mat"].shape
    V = cam.view()
    Lh = np.array([L[0], 0, L[2]]); Lh /= np.linalg.norm(Lh) + 1e-9
    pts, nrm, uu, vv, kind, inside = [], [], [], [], [], []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(P.get("seed", 0) + 99)
    cb = tr.crown_base
    for s in tr.wood:
        a, b, ra, rb = s["a"], s["b"], s["ra"], s["rb"]
        axis = b - a; ln = np.linalg.norm(axis)
        if ln < 1e-6:
            continue
        ax = axis / ln
        rmax = max(ra, rb)
        if s["kind"] == "limb" and rmax / mpp < P.get("limb_min_px", 0.35):
            continue
        if s["kind"] == "dead" and not s.get("trunk") and rmax / mpp < 0.2 and ln / mpp < 1.5:
            continue
        # frame
        e1 = np.cross(ax, V); nn = np.linalg.norm(e1)
        if nn < 1e-6:
            e1 = np.cross(ax, [1.0, 0, 0]); nn = np.linalg.norm(e1)
        e1 /= nn
        e2 = np.cross(e1, ax)       # toward viewer, perpendicular to axis
        nl = max(2, int(ln / (0.5 * mpp)) + 1)
        t = np.linspace(0, 1, nl)
        r = ra + (rb - ra) * t
        c = a + np.outer(t, axis)
        if rmax < 0.6 * mpp:
            nn_ = e2 + 0.8 * Lh; nn_ /= np.linalg.norm(nn_)
            pts.append(c); nrm.append(np.tile(nn_, (nl, 1)))
            uu.append(np.zeros(nl)); vv.append(c[:, 1] if s.get("trunk") else t * ln)
        else:
            na = max(3, int(np.pi * rmax / (0.5 * mpp)) + 1)
            th = np.linspace(-np.pi / 2, np.pi / 2, na)
            TT, TH = np.meshgrid(t, th, indexing="ij")
            R = (ra + (rb - ra) * TT)
            # buttress flutes: the radius ripples round the bole near the ground
            if s.get("trunk") and tr.sp.flutes > 0:
                yb = (a[1] + axis[1] * TT)
                fl = np.exp(-yb / (0.05 * tr.H + 0.6)) * tr.sp.buttress
                R = R * (1 + 0.18 * fl * np.cos(TH * tr.sp.flutes + 1.3) ** 3)
            C = a[None, None] + TT[..., None] * axis
            Nrm = np.cos(TH)[..., None] * e2 + np.sin(TH)[..., None] * e1
            pts.append((C + R[..., None] * Nrm).reshape(-1, 3))
            nrm.append(Nrm.reshape(-1, 3))
            uu.append((TH / np.pi).reshape(-1))
            vv.append(C[..., 1].reshape(-1) if s.get("trunk") else (TT * ln).reshape(-1))
        m = len(pts[-1])
        kind.append(np.full(m, {"bark": BARK, "limb": LIMB, "dead": DEAD}[s["kind"]]))
        inside.append(np.full(m, 1.0 if not s.get("trunk") else 0.0))
    if not pts:
        return
    Pp = np.concatenate(pts); Nn = np.concatenate(nrm); U = np.concatenate(uu); Vv = np.concatenate(vv)
    Kd = np.concatenate(kind)
    sx, sy, dep = cam.project(Pp)
    xs = np.floor(sx + origin[0] + 0.5).astype(np.int64); ys = np.floor(sy + origin[1] + 0.5).astype(np.int64)
    lam = np.clip(Nn @ L, 0, 1)
    # shade inside the crown: bole and limbs under the canopy get little sun
    y = Pp[:, 1]
    crown_len = max(tr.top_y - cb, 1)
    inn = np.clip((y - cb) / max(0.6, 0.06 * tr.H), 0, 1) * (y < tr.top_y + 0.5)
    shade = np.where(Kd == DEAD, 0.0, inn)
    sunlit = lam * (1 - P.get("wood_shade", 0.85) * shade)
    amb = 0.3 * (1 - 0.8 * shade) * (0.6 + 0.4 * Nn[:, 1] + 0.2)
    ok = (xs >= 0) & (xs < W) & (ys >= 0) & (ys < H)
    xs, ys, dep, sunlit, amb, U, Vv, Kd = xs[ok], ys[ok], dep[ok], sunlit[ok], amb[ok], U[ok], Vv[ok], Kd[ok]
    o = np.argsort(dep, kind="stable")
    xs, ys, dep, sunlit, amb, U, Vv, Kd = xs[o], ys[o], dep[o], sunlit[o], amb[o], U[o], Vv[o], Kd[o]
    front = dep > G["dep"][ys, xs]
    xs, ys, dep, sunlit, amb, U, Vv, Kd = xs[front], ys[front], dep[front], sunlit[front], amb[front], U[front], Vv[front], Kd[front]
    G["mat"][ys, xs] = Kd
    G["dep"][ys, xs] = dep
    G["dir"][ys, xs] = sunlit
    G["amb"][ys, xs] = amb
    G["u"][ys, xs] = U
    G["v"][ys, xs] = Vv


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ quantization

def bark_pattern(kind, u, v, mpp, rng_seed, H):
    """A step offset field for the bark, designed in pixels (nothing finer than ~2 px).
    u: -0.5..0.5 around the visible half of the bole; v: height in metres."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    # pattern space scaled so features stay >= ~2 px
    px_u = u  # fraction of visible circumference
    if kind == "furrow":      # Douglas-fir: deep vertical furrows, thick corky ridges, braided
        ph = rng.uniform(0, 6.28, 3)
        f = np.sin(px_u * 9 * np.pi + 1.2 * np.sin(v * 0.35 + ph[0]) + 0.6 * np.sin(v * 1.1 + ph[1]))
        return np.where(f > 0.55, -2, np.where(f > 0.2, -1, 0)) + np.where(f < -0.7, 1, 0)
    if kind == "fibrous":     # cedar: long fibrous strips, fine vertical lines, low relief
        f = np.sin(px_u * 16 * np.pi + 0.8 * np.sin(v * 0.2) + 0.3 * np.sin(v * 0.9 + u * 5))
        return np.where(f > 0.75, -1, 0) + np.where(f < -0.85, 1, 0)
    if kind == "scaly":       # hemlock: scaly, shallow furrows
        f = np.sin(px_u * 10 * np.pi + np.sin(v * 1.5) * 0.9) * np.sin(v * 2.2 + px_u * 3)
        return np.where(f > 0.45, -1, 0) + np.where(f < -0.6, 1, 0)
    if kind == "plates":      # Sitka: thin scaly plates, loose flakes
        f = np.sin(px_u * 12 * np.pi + 2 * np.floor(v * 1.8)) * np.sin(v * 3.6)
        return np.where(f > 0.5, -1, 0) + np.where(f < -0.55, 1, 0)
    return 0


def quantize(G, P, tr=None, mpp=0.1):
    """-> step canvas. Foliage steps 0..7 (0-3 shadow family, 4-7 lit family);
    bark/limb/dead steps 0..5."""
    mat = G["mat"]
    d, a = G["dir"], G["amb"]
    thr = P.get("lit_thr", 0.14)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(P.get("seed", 0) + 5)
    Hh, W = mat.shape
    r = rng.random((Hh, W)); r2 = rng.random((Hh, W))
    # optional spatial coherence of the mixture (clumped light on sunlit tufts)
    if P.get("mix_grain", 0) > 0:
        g = gaussian_filter(rng.random((Hh, W)), P["mix_grain"])
        g = (g - g.mean()) / (g.std() + 1e-9)
        r = np.clip(0.5 + 0.29 * (0.55 * g + 0.45 * (r - 0.5) / 0.29), 0, 0.9999)
    mark = G["mark"]
    lit = d > thr
    v = np.clip((d - thr) / P.get("lit_span", 0.5), 0, 1)
    v = np.clip(v + np.where(mark, P.get("mark_bias", 0.2), -0.05), 0, 1)
    pl = P.get("p_lit", 0.62) * v ** P.get("g_lit", 0.9)
    pd = P.get("p_dol", 0.6) * (1 - 0.5 * v) * np.clip(v * 3 + 0.3, 0, 1)
    s_lit = np.where(r < pl, np.where(r2 < v * 0.6, 7, 6), np.where(r < pl + pd, np.where(r2 < 0.5, 5, 4), 0))
    sv = np.clip(a / P.get("amb_span", 0.35), 0, 1)
    ps = P.get("p_fleck", 0.25) * sv + np.where(mark, 0.08, 0)
    s_sh = np.where(r < ps, np.where(r2 < 0.5, 3, 2), np.where(r < ps * 2.2, 1, 0))
    step = np.where(lit, s_lit, s_sh).astype(np.int16)
    step[mat != FOL] = 0
    # wood: 6 steps from light, with the bark pattern as step offsets
    wood = (mat == BARK) | (mat == LIMB) | (mat == DEAD)
    if wood.any():
        wv = np.clip(d * P.get("wood_kd", 0.9) + a, 0, 0.999)
        ws = np.floor(wv * 6 + (r - 0.5) * P.get("wood_dither", 0.7))
        if tr is not None:
            pat = bark_pattern(tr.sp.bark, G["u"], G["v"], mpp, P.get("seed", 0), tr.H)
            ws = ws + np.where(mat == BARK, pat, 0)
            dpat = bark_pattern("fibrous", G["u"], G["v"], mpp, 3, tr.H)
            ws = ws + np.where(mat == DEAD, dpat, 0)
        step = np.where(wood, ws.clip(0, 5), step).astype(np.int16)
    step[mat == NONE] = -1
    return step


def compose(step, mat, pal, bg):
    out = bg.copy()
    for m, key in ((FOL, "fol"), (BARK, "bark"), (LIMB, "limb"), (DEAD, "dead"), (MOSS, "moss")):
        sel = (mat == m) & (step >= 0)
        if sel.any():
            ramp = pal[key]
            out[sel] = ramp[np.clip(step[sel], 0, len(ramp) - 1)]
    return out


def paint_tree(tr, mpp, W, H, origin, sun=(20, 35), el=0.0, yaw=0.0, P=None):
    P = dict(P or {})
    cam = Cam(mpp, el, yaw)
    L = sun_vec(*sun)
    G = rasterize(tr, cam, L, W, H, origin, P)
    st = quantize(G, P, tr, mpp)
    return G, st
Bash: Stage 1 greyscale four species
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/conifers; mkdir -p stage1; cat > s1_grey.py <<'EOF'
"""Stage 1: one tree of each species as a greyscale mass (values from Ferrari's conifer ramp)."""
import numpy as np, sys, time
from PIL import Image
import grow, paint, cmp
g=lambda v: np.array([[x,x,x] for x in v],np.uint8)
PAL=dict(fol=g([0,12,22,40,78,95,150,175]), bark=g([20,40,62,85,110,135]), limb=g([15,30,48,66,85,100]),
         dead=g([70,100,130,160,185,210]), moss=g([60,90,120,150,180,200]))
def cell(sp,age,seed,mpp,W,H,sun=(20,35),P=None,bgv=200):
    t0=time.time()
    tr=grow.grow(sp,age,seed=seed,mpp=mpp)
    G,st=paint.paint_tree(tr,mpp,W,H,(W/2,H-3),sun=sun,P=P or {})
    bg=np.full((H,W,3),bgv,np.uint8)
    img=paint.compose(st,G['mat'],PAL,bg)
    print(sp,age,len(tr.fol['p']),f'{time.time()-t0:.1f}s')
    return img
if __name__=='__main__':
    tag=sys.argv[1]
    rows=[]
    for age,mpp,W,H in [('young',0.07,130,220),('mature',0.2,130,220)]:
        cells=[cell(sp,age,1,mpp,W,H) for sp in ['douglas','hemlock','cedar','spruce']]
        rows.append(cmp.panel(cells,[f'{sp} {age}' for sp in ['douglas','hemlock','cedar','spruce']],k=2))
    cmp.stack(rows).save(f'stage1/{tag}.png')

Write: elements/conifers/palette.py
"""The palette is the light (SYNTHESIS). Ramps per material, per hour and weather, per depth band.

Foliage ramps have 8 steps: 0-3 shadow family (sky-lit, hue walks toward blue-black), 4-7 lit
family (sun-lit, hue walks from brown-olive at the dark end to the sun's colour at the top), after
Ferrari's V16 conifer ramp: black, (0,7,0), (0,11,0), (0,31,15) | (95,71,0), (79,83,19),
(143,151,35), (151,163,63).
Wood ramps have 6 steps. Depth (aerial perspective) mixes every step toward the horizon sky and
lifts the black point; the lit colour holds longer than the shadow colour (master-copy finding:
the lit colour stays constant with distance, the shadow colour changes).
"""
import numpy as np

# light states: sun colour, sky (ambient) colour, sun strength, sky gradient top/horizon
HOURS = {
    "dawn":     dict(sun=(255, 170, 120), sky=(120, 120, 190), ks=0.8, top=(70, 80, 150), hor=(245, 175, 150), el=6),
    "morning":  dict(sun=(255, 236, 170), sky=(110, 150, 230), ks=1.0, top=(20, 150, 250), hor=(120, 215, 255), el=25),
    "noon":     dict(sun=(255, 250, 235), sky=(130, 170, 235), ks=1.05, top=(60, 130, 220), hor=(175, 215, 245), el=58),
    "golden":   dict(sun=(255, 190, 90), sky=(120, 120, 180), ks=1.0, top=(90, 110, 190), hor=(250, 200, 140), el=10),
    "dusk":     dict(sun=(250, 130, 110), sky=(110, 95, 160), ks=0.55, top=(60, 55, 120), hor=(235, 150, 150), el=3),
    "overcast": dict(sun=(200, 205, 205), sky=(175, 185, 190), ks=0.0, top=(160, 168, 175), hor=(205, 210, 212), el=40),
    "fog":      dict(sun=(220, 220, 215), sky=(190, 196, 196), ks=0.0, top=(185, 190, 190), hor=(215, 218, 216), el=40),
    "moon":     dict(sun=(150, 170, 220), sky=(30, 40, 80), ks=0.45, top=(8, 12, 35), hor=(35, 50, 90), el=35),
}

SPECIES_ALB = {
    # foliage albedo (sRGB-ish), lit-tip tint (new growth / sheen), shadow hue bias
    "douglas":   dict(alb=(70, 90, 38), tip=(160, 170, 70), cool=0.2),
    "hemlock":   dict(alb=(48, 82, 42), tip=(130, 170, 80), cool=0.25),
    "cedar":     dict(alb=(92, 118, 40), tip=(185, 190, 80), cool=0.1),
    "spruce":    dict(alb=(58, 92, 88), tip=(150, 185, 175), cool=0.45),
    "subalpine": dict(alb=(64, 84, 36), tip=(150, 160, 60), cool=0.2),
}
BARK_ALB = {
    "douglas": (98, 70, 55),     # dark red-brown ridges, grey-brown
    "hemlock": (95, 72, 62),
    "cedar":   (120, 80, 60),    # reddish-brown to silver-grey strips
    "spruce":  (110, 98, 88),    # grey-brown thin plates
}


def _lin(c):
    c = np.asarray(c, float) / 255
    return np.where(c <= 0.04045, c / 12.92, ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4)


def _srgb(l):
    l = np.clip(l, 0, 1)
    return np.round(255 * np.where(l <= 0.0031308, 12.92 * l, 1.055 * l ** (1 / 2.4) - 0.055)).astype(np.uint8)


def foliage_ramp(species, hour, depth=0.0, fog=0.0):
    h = HOURS[hour]; s = SPECIES_ALB.get(species, SPECIES_ALB["douglas"])
    A = _lin(s["alb"]); TIP = _lin(s["tip"])
    S = _lin(h["sun"]); K = _lin(h["sky"])
    ks = h["ks"]
    out = []
    # shadow family: sky light only, falling into the crown's dark; hue walks toward the sky colour
    for a in (0.0, 0.035, 0.08, 0.2):
        c = A * K * a * 1.6
        c = c * (1 - s["cool"] * 0.4) + K * a * 0.12 * s["cool"]
        out.append(c)
    if ks > 0:
        # lit family: sun on needles; the dark end is warm brown-olive, the top takes the tip tint
        for b, warm in ((0.3, 0.6), (0.42, 0.3), (0.8, 0.0), (1.0, 0.0)):
            base = A * (S * b * ks * 1.9 + K * 0.25)
            if warm > 0:     # Ferrari's (95,71,0): sun-warmed dark olive leaning to brown
                base = base * np.array([1 + 0.5 * warm, 1.0, 1 - 0.8 * warm])
            if b >= 0.8:
                t = 0.35 if b < 1 else 0.6
                base = base * (1 - t) + TIP * S * b * ks * t * 1.2
            out.append(base)
    else:
        # overcast/fog: no sun family; the "lit" steps are the sky-lit tops, low contrast
        for b in (0.3, 0.38, 0.5, 0.6):
            out.append(A * K * b * 1.2 + TIP * K * b * 0.15)
    ramp = np.array(out)
    ramp = _aerial(ramp, hour, depth, fog)
    return _srgb(ramp)


def wood_ramp(kind, species, hour, depth=0.0, fog=0.0):
    h = HOURS[hour]
    if kind == "dead":
        A = _lin((175, 172, 165))      # silvered snag wood
    elif kind == "limb":
        A = _lin(BARK_ALB.get(species, (95, 75, 60))) * 0.8
    elif kind == "moss":
        A = _lin((120, 150, 40))
    else:
        A = _lin(BARK_ALB.get(species, (95, 75, 60)))
    S = _lin(h["sun"]); K = _lin(h["sky"])
    out = []
    for i, v in enumerate((0.03, 0.08, 0.16, 0.3, 0.55, 0.85)):
        sunpart = max(0, (i - 2)) / 3
        c = A * (K * v * 0.9 * (1 - sunpart * 0.5) + S * v * h["ks"] * sunpart * 1.4)
        out.append(c)
    return _srgb(_aerial(np.array(out), hour, depth, fog))


def _aerial(ramp, hour, depth, fog):
    """Aerial perspective: mix toward the horizon colour; darks lift most, lights hold."""
    if depth <= 0 and fog <= 0:
        return ramp
    h = HOURS[hour]
    Hc = _lin(h["hor"]) * 0.8 + _lin(h["top"]) * 0.2
    lum = ramp @ np.array([0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722])
    dark = 1 - np.clip(lum / (lum.max() + 1e-6), 0, 1)
    amt = np.clip(depth * (0.55 + 0.45 * dark) + fog, 0, 0.97)[:, None]
    # slight violet bias in the far darks (analysis: the black point lifts and turns violet)
    vio = np.array([1.05, 0.95, 1.1])
    return ramp * (1 - amt) + Hc * vio * amt


def palette(species, hour, depth=0.0, fog=0.0):
    return dict(fol=foliage_ramp(species, hour, depth, fog), bark=wood_ramp("bark", species, hour, depth, fog),
                limb=wood_ramp("limb", species, hour, depth, fog), dead=wood_ramp("dead", species, hour, depth, fog),
                moss=wood_ramp("moss", species, hour, depth, fog))


def sky(hour, H, W, seed=0, bands=8):
    """Ferrari's sky: flat bands joined by a 1-2 row checker seam."""
    h = HOURS[hour]
    top = np.array(h["top"], float); hor = np.array(h["hor"], float)
    y = np.linspace(0, 1, H)[:, None]
    cols = [top * (1 - t) + hor * t for t in np.linspace(0, 1, bands)]
    band = np.clip((y[:, 0] ** 1.3) * (bands - 1), 0, bands - 1)
    b0 = np.floor(band).astype(int); fr = band - b0
    out = np.zeros((H, W, 3), np.uint8)
    xx = np.arange(W)
    for r in range(H):
        c0 = np.array(cols[b0[r]]); c1 = np.array(cols[min(b0[r] + 1, bands - 1)])
        row = np.tile(c0, (W, 1))
        if fr[r] > 0.75:       # checker seam before the next band
            m = (xx + r) % 2 == 0
            row[m] = c1
        out[r] = row
    return out

"""Stage 1b: species review: photo | mine (grey) | mine (colour, morning)."""
import numpy as np, sys, time
from PIL import Image
import grow, paint, palette, cmp, s1_grey
REF='refs/'
PHOTOS={'douglas':'lone_Douglas_fir_tree/openverse_Edworthy_Park_Douglas_Fir_f1516998.jpg',
        'hemlock':'Tsuga_heterophylla/commons_Hoh_Rainforest_Olympic_National_Park_Washington__8f0608f4.jpg',
        'cedar':'Thuja_plicata/commons_Thuja_plicata_kz01_jpg_65cfc9a2.jpg',
        'spruce':'Douglas_fir_tree_crown/openverse_Sitka_Spruce_b36ebb43.jpg'}
def photo(sp,H):
    im=Image.open(REF+PHOTOS[sp]).convert('RGB'); w=int(im.width*H/im.height)
    return np.asarray(im.resize((w,H),Image.LANCZOS))
def mine(sp,age,seed,mpp,W,H,hour='morning',P=None,sun=None,**kw):
    tr=grow.grow(sp,age,seed=seed,mpp=mpp,**kw)
    hh=palette.HOURS[hour]
    sun=sun or (20,hh['el'])
    G,st=paint.paint_tree(tr,mpp,W,H,(W/2,H-3),sun=sun,P=P or {})
    bg=palette.sky(hour,H,W)
    col=paint.compose(st,G['mat'],palette.palette(sp,hour),bg)
    grey=paint.compose(st,G['mat'],s1_grey.PAL,np.full((H,W,3),200,np.uint8))
    return grey,col
if __name__=='__main__':
    tag=sys.argv[1]; age=sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv)>2 else 'mature'
    mpp=dict(sapling=0.02,young=0.08,mature=0.2,old=0.3)[age]
    rows=[]
    for sp in ['douglas','hemlock','cedar','spruce']:
        g,c=mine(sp,age,1,mpp,140,230)
        rows.append(cmp.panel([photo(sp,230),g,c],[f'{sp} photo',f'{age} grey','morning'],k=2))
    cmp.stack(rows).save(f'stage1/{tag}.png')
EOF

Bash: Element-coherent mixture
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/conifers; python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('paint.py').read()
old='''    mark = G["mark"]
    lit = d > thr
    v = np.clip((d - thr) / P.get("lit_span", 0.5), 0, 1)'''
new='''    # element coherence: at near scales each spray takes one mixture draw, so sprays read as
    # marks; the per-pixel draw survives in a fraction of pixels (texture inside the spray)
    eid = G["eid"]
    if P.get("elem_coh", 0.75) > 0:
        he = ((eid * 2654435761) % 1000003) / 1000003.0
        he2 = ((eid * 40503 + 12345) % 999983) / 999983.0
        use = (rng.random((Hh, W)) < P.get("elem_coh", 0.75)) & (eid >= 0)
        r = np.where(use, he, r); r2 = np.where(use, he2, r2)
    mark = G["mark"]
    lit = d > thr
    v = np.clip((d - thr) / P.get("lit_span", 0.5), 0, 1)
    # light cap along each element: the base/top of a spray catches more light than its tip
    v = np.clip(v + P.get("cap_k", 0.25) * (0.5 - G["ek"]) * (G["kind"] >= 0), 0, 1)'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open('paint.py','w').write(s)
EOF

Bash: Wedge taper and olive palette shift
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/conifers; python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('grow.py').read()
old='''        taper = np.clip(1.15 - 0.6 * ((us - s0) / max(1 - s0, 1e-3)) ** 2, 0.4, 1.2) * elscale'''
new='''        xx = (us - s0) / max(1 - s0, 1e-3)
        taper = (np.clip(1 - xx, 0, 1) ** sp.wedge * 0.9 + 0.18) * elscale     # wedge: long sprays inboard, short at the tip'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace('''    shelf: float = 0.5            # how flat the shelves are (1 = flat tiers, 0 = rounded clumps)''','''    shelf: float = 0.5            # how flat the shelves are (1 = flat tiers, 0 = rounded clumps)
    wedge: float = 0.7            # spray length falloff toward the limb tip (pointed tier tips)''')
open('grow.py','w').write(s)
s=open('palette.py').read()
old='''        for b, warm in ((0.3, 0.6), (0.42, 0.3), (0.8, 0.0), (1.0, 0.0)):
            base = A * (S * b * ks * 1.9 + K * 0.25)
            if warm > 0:     # Ferrari's (95,71,0): sun-warmed dark olive leaning to brown
                base = base * np.array([1 + 0.5 * warm, 1.0, 1 - 0.8 * warm])
            if b >= 0.8:
                t = 0.35 if b < 1 else 0.6
                base = base * (1 - t) + TIP * S * b * ks * t * 1.2
            out.append(base)'''
new='''        # painterly shift: lit needles lean toward olive/ochre (Ferrari paints sunlit conifer
        # olive, not leaf green); `olive` is how far the lit family is pulled toward it
        OL = _lin((150, 150, 45))
        for b, warm in ((0.3, 0.7), (0.42, 0.35), (0.8, 0.0), (1.0, 0.0)):
            base = A * (S * b * ks * 1.9 + K * 0.25)
            lum = base @ np.array([0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722])
            ol = OL / (OL @ np.array([0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722])) * lum * S / S.max()
            base = base * (1 - s["olive"]) + ol * s["olive"]
            if warm > 0:     # Ferrari's (95,71,0): sun-warmed dark olive leaning to brown
                base = base * np.array([1 + 0.45 * warm, 1.0, 1 - 0.85 * warm])
            if b >= 0.8:
                t = 0.3 if b < 1 else 0.5
                base = base * (1 - t) + TIP * S * b * ks * t * 1.2
            out.append(base)'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace('''    "douglas":   dict(alb=(70, 90, 38), tip=(160, 170, 70), cool=0.2),
    "hemlock":   dict(alb=(48, 82, 42), tip=(130, 170, 80), cool=0.25),
    "cedar":     dict(alb=(92, 118, 40), tip=(185, 190, 80), cool=0.1),
    "spruce":    dict(alb=(58, 92, 88), tip=(150, 185, 175), cool=0.45),
    "subalpine": dict(alb=(64, 84, 36), tip=(150, 160, 60), cool=0.2),''','''    "douglas":   dict(alb=(70, 90, 38), tip=(160, 165, 70), cool=0.2, olive=0.55),

52. elements/conifers/stage1/n3.png

Bash: Single limb study per species
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/conifers; cat > s1_branch.py <<'EOF'
"""Stage 1a: the smallest form -- one limb of each species, near scale, greyscale then colour.
Compared with close photos of each species' sprays."""
import numpy as np, sys
from PIL import Image
import grow, paint, palette, cmp, s1_grey
def limb_tree(sp_name, L=3.0, seed=0, mpp=0.02, age='mature', ang=0.0, az=0.0, fans=True):
    sp=grow.SP[sp_name]; rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    ag=grow.AGE[age]
    p0=np.array([0,0,0.0])
    b=grow._limb(p0, az, ang, L, sp, rng, 0.5, False, 20, age)
    br=[b]
    if fans:
        nf=int(round(sp.fans*ag['fan']))
        for k in range(nf):
            s0=rng.uniform(0.25,0.75); side=1 if k%2==0 else -1
            c=grow._limb(grow._pt(b['pts'],s0), az+side*sp.fan_angle*rng.uniform(0.6,1.3), ang*0.5-0.1*sp.droop, (1-s0)*L*rng.uniform(0.55,0.95), sp, rng, 0.5, False, 20, age, child=True)
            c['parent']=0; br.append(c)
    fol=grow._foliage(br, sp, rng, mpp, 1.0, 20, age, ag['el'])
    wood=[]
    for bi,bb in enumerate(br):
        pts=bb['pts']
        for i in range(len(pts)-1):
            s=i/(len(pts)-1)
            wood.append(dict(a=pts[i],b=pts[i+1],ra=bb['r']*(1-s*0.8),rb=bb['r']*(1-(s+1/13)*0.8),kind='limb',trunk=False))
    axis=np.array([[-0.2,-5,0],[-0.2,5,0.0]])
    tr=grow.Tree(sp,age,20.0,np.zeros((2,4)),wood,br,fol,-5,axis,lambda y: np.full(np.shape(y),L*1.1),5.0)
    return tr
def paint_limb(sp,mpp,W,H,hour='morning',seed=0,L=3.0,P=None,grey=False,view_az=0.0):
    tr=limb_tree(sp,L,seed,mpp)
    hh=palette.HOURS[hour]
    P=dict(P or {}); P.setdefault('limb_min_px',0.2)
    G,st=paint.paint_tree(tr,mpp,W,H,(8,H*0.35),sun=(20,hh['el']),P=P,yaw=view_az)
    if grey: return paint.compose(st,G['mat'],s1_grey.PAL,np.full((H,W,3),200,np.uint8))
    return paint.compose(st,G['mat'],palette.palette(sp,hour),palette.sky(hour,H,W))
REFS={'douglas':'refs/Pseudotsuga_menziesii_tree/commons_Pseudotsuga_menziesii_seed_cones_jpg_2e3bf25e.jpg',
      'hemlock':'refs/Tsuga_heterophylla/commons_Tsuga_heterophylla_kz11_jpg_ff64d5b7.jpg',
      'cedar':'refs/Thuja_plicata/commons_Thuja_plicata_RF_jpg_5d43937c.jpg',
      'spruce':'refs/Picea_sitchensis_tree/commons_Sitka_spruce_1547_jpg_8ef793bc.jpg'}
if __name__=='__main__':
    tag=sys.argv[1]; mpp=float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv)>2 else 0.02
    rows=[]
    for sp in ['douglas','hemlock','cedar','spruce']:
        W,H=170,110
        im=Image.open(REFS[sp]).convert('RGB'); w=int(im.width*H/im.height)
        ph=np.asarray(im.resize((w,H),Image.LANCZOS))
        g=paint_limb(sp,mpp,W,H,grey=True); c=paint_limb(sp,mpp,W,H)
        c2=paint_limb(sp,mpp,W,H,view_az=70)
        rows.append(cmp.panel([ph,g,c,c2],[f'{sp} photo','limb grey','colour','limb toward viewer'],k=2))
    cmp.stack(rows).save(f'stage1/{tag}.png')
EOF
OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 nice -n 10 python3 s1_branch.py b1 0.02

s=open('grow.py').read()

    needle: float = 0.015         # half-width of the leafy surface round each twig line (m)''')
# species needle widths
s=s.replace('''shelf=0.35, candelabra=0.1, spike=0.15),''','''shelf=0.35, candelabra=0.1, spike=0.15, needle=0.028),''')
s=s.replace('''trunk_frac=0.012, bark="scaly", buttress=0.2, shelf=0.6, spike=0.05),''','''trunk_frac=0.012, bark="scaly", buttress=0.2, shelf=0.6, spike=0.05, needle=0.016),''')

s=s.replace('''candelabra=0.15, spike=0.1),''','''candelabra=0.15, spike=0.1, needle=0.022),''')
# needle fill inside _element: wrap returns
s=s.replace('''def _element(kind, c, tang, side, radial, sp, rng, step, taper, L):
    """Sample one foliage element as points. Returns (P, N, D, ek)."""''','''def _element(kind, c, tang, side, radial, sp, rng, step, taper, L):
    """Sample one foliage element as points, then fill its leafy width (needles) where that
    width is over a pixel. Returns (P, N, D, ek)."""
    el = _element_lines(kind, c, tang, side, radial, sp, rng, step, taper, L)
    if el is None or sp.needle < step * 0.9:
        return el
    P, Nn, D, ek = el
    k = int(sp.needle / step)
    up = np.array([0, 1.0, 0])
    if kind == "frond":
        perp = np.cross(D, Nn)            # in the spray plane, across the twig
    else:
        perp = np.cross(D, radial + 0.3 * up)
    perp /= np.linalg.norm(perp, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9
    outs = [P]; ns = [Nn]; ds = [D]; es = [ek]
    for j in range(1, k + 1):
        for sg in (-1, 1):
            off = perp * (sg * j * step) + np.outer(np.full(len(P), -0.35 * j * step), up) + D * (0.4 * j * step)
            outs.append(P + off); ns.append(Nn); ds.append(D * 0.5 + perp * sg * 0.8 - up * 0.3); es.append(ek)
    return np.concatenate(outs), np.concatenate(ns), np.concatenate(ds), np.concatenate(es)


def _element_lines(kind, c, tang, side, radial, sp, rng, step, taper, L):''')

58. elements/conifers/stage2/a1.png

Bash: Area fill for coarse scales; dark dead limbs
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/conifers; python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('grow.py').read()
old='''    el = _element_lines(kind, c, tang, side, radial, sp, rng, step, taper, L)
    if el is None or sp.needle < step * 0.9:
        return el
    P, Nn, D, ek = el'''
new='''    el = _element_lines(kind, c, tang, side, radial, sp, rng, step, taper, L)
    if el is None:
        return el
    P, Nn, D, ek = el
    # coarse scales: the spray's leafy area (width across its twig) is under a few pixels, so the
    # line skeleton would read as sticks; fill the area with scattered points instead
    if kind == "frond":
        hw = sp.pinna * sp.frond_len * taper * 0.8
        drawn = sp.pinna * sp.frond_len * taper > step * 1.2
    elif kind == "strand":
        hw = 0.12 * sp.strand_len * taper * sp.strand_fan + sp.needle
        drawn = False
    else:
        hw = 0.5 * sp.brush_len * taper
        drawn = False
    if (not drawn) and hw > 0.5 * step and step > 2 * sp.needle:
        up = np.array([0, 1.0, 0])
        perp = np.cross(D, Nn if kind == "frond" else (radial + 0.3 * up))
        perp /= np.linalg.norm(perp, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9
        nf = int(min(6, 0.8 * 2 * hw / step)) + 1
        idx = rng.integers(0, len(P), len(P) * nf)
        o = rng.uniform(-hw, hw, len(idx))
        Pf = P[idx] + perp[idx] * o[:, None] - np.outer(np.abs(o) * 0.3, up)
        P = np.concatenate([P, Pf]); Nn = np.concatenate([Nn, Nn[idx]]); D = np.concatenate([D, D[idx]]); ek = np.concatenate([ek, ek[idx]])
    if sp.needle < step * 0.9:
        return P, Nn, D, ek'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
# dead lower limbs are dark limb wood, not silver
s=s.replace('''                             kind="dead" if b["dead"] else "limb", trunk=False, bid=bi))''','''                             kind="limb", trunk=False, bid=bi, dead=b["dead"]))''')
open('grow.py','w').write(s)
EOF
OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 nice -n 10 python3 s2_ages.py a2 >/dev/null

## 6. For the painters: each tree at every distance

Colours below are starting points in sRGB, from photographs in normal overcast-to-sunny light;
treat them as a palette to check against the photos in `photos/`, not as gospel. West-side
light is often **soft, overcast and blue-grey**, which flattens value contrast and makes the
conifers read almost black-green; in low winter sun the canopy tops catch warm light and the
interior drops to deep blue-green.

### Douglas-fir: the tree of the landscape

- **Young (under 40 y):** a full symmetrical cone to the ground in the open; in plantations a
  dense even stand of narrow cones with dead lower branches. Upswept branch tips.
- **Mature (80 to 200 y):** tall straight column; the crown occupies the top third to half; a
  **long clean bole** below, with dead branch stubs.
- **Old (300+ y):** the silhouette changes completely. The top is **flat, broken or
  irregular** (tops snap in storms and are replaced by one or more upturned limbs), the
  crown is **ragged and open with big gaps and heavy horizontal limbs**, often **only in the
  top 10 to 20 m of a 60 to 70 m tree**. From below you see a pillar of furrowed bark with no
  branches for 30 to 40 m.
- **Bark:** thick, **deeply furrowed**, dark grey-brown ridges (#4a3e36 to #5f5249) with
  cinnamon-red to rust in the furrows (#7a4a30). Young bark is smooth grey-brown with resin
  blisters.
- **Foliage:** medium to dark green (shadow #1e3222, mid #2f4a2c, light #4f6b3a), needles all
  round the twig so sprays look soft but "bottle-brush". Cones 5 to 10 cm, hanging, with
  three-pointed bracts (the hind feet and tail of a mouse hiding in the cone, in a widely told Native story).
- **At 100 m to 1 km:** old-growth Douglas-fir forest is **lumpy and deeply shadowed**:
  emergent crowns stand above the rest, with dark holes between. Plantation Douglas-fir is
  smooth and evenly textured.
- **Seasonal:** bright lime-green new shoots in May to June (#9fc25a), which dull over summer.

### Western hemlock: the shade tree

- **The drooping leader.** Every hemlock's tip nods over like a bent fishing rod. This is the
  single fastest way to tell it from Douglas-fir at 100 m, and it should be in the grammar.
- Branches droop slightly with sprays hanging down; the crown is **soft, feathery,
  fine-textured**, darker and more even than Douglas-fir (shadow #17291f, mid #253f2c).
- Old hemlocks keep crowns lower down the stem (they tolerate their own shade), often with
  **mistletoe brooms** (dense witches'-broom clumps) and **conks** on the trunk.
- **Bark:** thinner, reddish-brown to grey-brown, finely scaly, becoming furrowed on old trees.
- **Regeneration:** carpets of hemlock seedlings on every rotting log; **lines of hemlocks
  standing on stilt roots** where a nurse log has rotted away ("colonnades"; guide 05).

### Western redcedar: the elder

- **Base:** broadly **flared, buttressed and fluted**, often hollow (a cave you can stand in).
- **Bark:** fibrous, **st

Write: elements/conifers/conifer.py
"""Public API: paint one Pacific Northwest conifer, or a stand of them.

    import conifer
    rgb, info = conifer.tree("cedar", "old", seed=3, mpp=0.25, hour="golden", el=0, W=200, H=320)
    rgb = conifer.stand([...placements...], mpp=1.0, hour="morning", el=30, W=..., H=...)

Parameters (all have defaults):
  species   douglas | hemlock | cedar | spruce
  age       sapling | young | mature | old
  seed      individual
  mpp       metres per pixel (scene ~0.03-0.08, landscape ~0.2-0.5, region ~1-4)
  hour      dawn | morning | noon | golden | dusk | overcast | fog | moon  (palette.HOURS)
  sun_az    0 = from the left, 180 = from the right, 270 = behind the tree (contre-jour)
  el        camera elevation, 0 = side view, 30 = the iso view
  depth     aerial-perspective amount 0..1 (palette); fog adds a flat veil
  P         painter parameters (paint.py): lit_frac (value plan), mix probabilities, marks ...
The index canvas (material, step) is returned in info so an hour change is a palette swap.
"""
import numpy as np
import grow, paint, palette

DEFAULT_P = dict(lit_frac=0.42, mark_frac=0.35, mark_m=0.25, p_lit=0.62, p_dol=0.6, elem_coh=0.75)


def scale_P(P, mpp):
    """Mark handoff by scale: element coherence and stroke length fade as sprays go sub-pixel."""
    P = dict(DEFAULT_P, **(P or {}))
    if "elem_coh" not in (P.get("_user") or {}):
        P["elem_coh"] = float(np.clip(0.9 - 0.8 * (mpp - 0.03) / 0.3, 0.15, 0.9))
    return P


def tree(species="douglas", age="mature", seed=0, mpp=0.2, hour="morning", sun_az=20.0, el=0.0, yaw=0.0,
         W=160, H=260, origin=None, depth=0.0, fog=0.0, P=None, bg=None, openness=0.6, lean=(0, 0), tr=None,
         sky=True, **grow_kw):
    hh = palette.HOURS[hour]
    tr = tr or grow.grow(species, age, seed=seed, mpp=mpp, openness=openness, lean=lean, **grow_kw)
    P = scale_P(P, mpp)
    P.setdefault("seed", seed)
    if hh["ks"] == 0:          # overcast: no sun family; light from the sky dome above
        P = dict(P, lit_frac=P.get("lit_frac_overcast", 0.3), wrap=0.6)
    origin = origin or (W / 2, H - 3)
    G, st = paint.paint_tree(tr, mpp, W, H, origin, sun=(sun_az, max(hh["el"], 4)), el=el, yaw=yaw, P=P)
    if bg is None:
        bg = palette.sky(hour, H, W) if sky else np.zeros((H, W, 3), np.uint8)
    pal = palette.palette(species, hour, depth, fog)
    rgb = paint.compose(st, G["mat"], pal, bg)
    return rgb, dict(G=G, step=st, tree=tr, pal=pal)


def fit_mpp(species, age, seed, Hpx, margin=0.9, **kw):
    tr = grow.grow(species, age, seed=seed, mpp=1.0, **kw)
    top = tr.H + 1.0
    return top / (Hpx * margin), tr

# pad to same height, bottom aligned

    pad=np.full((Hm,c.shape[1],3),250,np.uint8); pad[Hm-c.shape[0]:]=c; out.append(pad)

Bash: Add stand compositing with shared shadow map
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/conifers; grep -n "direct = lam \* T" paint.py; cat >> conifer.py <<'EOF'


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ stands
import copy
_CACHE = {}


def grown(species, age, seed, mpp, **kw):
    key = (species, age, seed, round(np.log2(mpp) * 4) / 4, tuple(sorted(kw.items())))
    if key not in _CACHE:
        _CACHE[key] = grow.grow(species, age, seed=seed, mpp=mpp, **kw)
    return _CACHE[key]


def placed(tr, x, z, yaw=0.0, y=0.0, hscale=1.0):
    """A copy of a grown tree turned by yaw (radians) and set down at (x, y, z)."""
    c, s = np.cos(yaw), np.sin(yaw)
    R = np.array([[c, 0, s], [0, 1, 0], [-s, 0, c]]) * np.array([1, 1, 1])
    off = np.array([x, y, z])
    t = copy.copy(tr)
    f = dict(tr.fol)
    f["p"] = tr.fol["p"] @ R.T + off
    f["n"] = tr.fol["n"] @ R.T
    f["d"] = tr.fol["d"] @ R.T
    t.fol = f
    t.wood = [dict(w, a=w["a"] @ R.T + off, b=w["b"] @ R.T + off) for w in tr.wood]
    t.axis = tr.axis @ R.T + off
    rf, yy = tr.rfun, y
    t.rfun = lambda q, rf=rf, yy=yy: rf(q - yy)
    t.crown_base = tr.crown_base + y
    t.top_y = tr.top_y + y
    t.trunk = tr.trunk.copy(); t.trunk[:, :3] = tr.trunk[:, :3] @ R.T + off
    return t


def stand(trees, mpp, W, H, hour="morning", sun_az=20.0, el=0.0, origin=None, P=None, far=300.0,
          ground=None, stand_shadow=True, fog=0.0, bg=None):
    """trees: list of dict(species, age, seed, x, z, yaw, y). Depth-composited; one shared
    opacity shadow map along the sun so trees shade each other (the dark forest interior)."""
    hh = palette.HOURS[hour]
    L = paint.sun_vec(sun_az, max(hh["el"], 4))
    P = scale_P(P, mpp)
    origin = origin or (W / 2, H - 3)
    objs = []
    for i, d in enumerate(trees):
        tr = grown(d["species"], d["age"], d.get("seed", i), mpp)
        objs.append((d, placed(tr, d["x"], d["z"], d.get("yaw", 0.0), d.get("y", 0.0))))
    if stand_shadow and len(objs) > 1:
        allp = np.concatenate([t.fol["p"] for _, t in objs])
        cell = max(mpp * 2.0, 0.3); step = max(0.45 * mpp, 0.015)
        Tall = paint.shadow_T(allp, L, cell, P.get("shadow_k", 0.5) * step ** 2 / cell ** 2)
        k0 = 0
        for d, t in objs:
            n = len(t.fol["p"]); d["_T"] = Tall[k0:k0 + n]; k0 += n
    out = palette.sky(hour, H, W) if bg is None else bg.copy()
    dep = np.full((H, W), -1e9)
    cam = paint.Cam(mpp, el)
    idx_mat = np.zeros((H, W), np.int16); idx_step = np.full((H, W), -1, np.int16)
    for d, t in objs:
        Pi = dict(P, seed=d.get("seed", 0) * 31 + 7)
        if "_T" in d:
            Pi["T_ext"] = d["_T"]
        if hh["ks"] == 0:
            Pi.update(lit_frac=P.get("lit_frac_overcast", 0.3), wrap=0.6)
        G = paint.rasterize(t, cam, L, W, H, origin, Pi)
        st = paint.quantize(G, Pi, t, mpp)
        dz = -d["z"]
        depth = float(np.clip(dz / far, 0, 0.9)) if far else 0.0
        pal = palette.palette(d["species"], hour, depth, fog)
        rgb = paint.compose(st, G["mat"], pal, out)
        m = (G["mat"] > 0) & (G["dep"] > dep)
        out[m] = rgb[m]; dep[m] = G["dep"][m]
        idx_mat[m] = G["mat"][m]; idx_step[m] = st[m]
    return out, dict(dep=dep, mat=idx_mat, step=idx_step)
EOF

"""Stage 4: several together -> a mass: stands of mixed conifers, side view and iso."""
import numpy as np, sys, time
import conifer, cmp
def layout(n, w, dmin, dmax, seed, mix=('douglas','hemlock','cedar','spruce'), ages=('mature','old','young')):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); T=[]
    for i in range(n):
        T.append(dict(species=mix[rng.integers(len(mix))], age=ages[rng.integers(len(ages))] if rng.random()<0.8 else 'mature',
                      seed=int(rng.integers(0,6)), x=rng.uniform(-w/2,w/2), z=-rng.uniform(dmin,dmax), yaw=rng.uniform(0,6.28)))
    T.sort(key=lambda d:d['z'])
    return T
if __name__=='__main__':
    tag=sys.argv[1]; mpp=float(sys.argv[2]); el=float(sys.argv[3]); n=int(sys.argv[4])
    W,H=int(sys.argv[5]),int(sys.argv[6]); hour=sys.argv[7] if len(sys.argv)>7 else 'morning'
    t0=time.time()
    T=layout(n, W*mpp*1.1, 0, 60 if el==0 else H*mpp*1.5, 3)
    org=(W/2, H-4 if el==0 else H*0.95)
    rgb,_=conifer.stand(T,mpp,W,H,hour=hour,el=el,origin=org,far=250)
    print(f'{time.time()-t0:.0f}s')
    cmp.panel([rgb],[f'{n} trees {mpp}m/px el{el} {hour}'],k=int(sys.argv[8]) if len(sys.argv)>8 else 2).save(f'stage4/{tag}.png')
EOF
